        1  # Klipper for Mac
        2  
        3 -*project codename: MaKlipper — the app is Klipper for Mac*

          -c becomes                                                          
        7 -the host. No Linux box, no SD card, no hard fork.                  
        5 +Run [Klipper](https://www.klipper3d.org) on any Mac — Intel or Appl
          +e Silicon —                                                        
        6 +like it were the Raspberry Pi. Plug your printer board into USB, an
          +d the Mac                                                          
        7 +becomes the host. No Linux box, no SD card, no fork: Klipper, Moonr
          +aker, Mainsail                                                     
        8 +and Fluidd are downloaded **pristine from upstream** at pinned vers
          +ions. macOS                                                        
        9 +support comes entirely from two small compatibility headers injecte
          +d at compile                                                       
       10 +time; upstream files are never edited (`klipperformac doctor` prove
          +s it with                                                          
       11 +`git status`).                                                     
       12  
        9 -**MaKlipper is not a Klipper fork.** Klipper, Moonraker, Mainsail a
          -nd Fluidd are                                                      
       10 -downloaded pristine from upstream GitHub at setup time, at pinned r
          -efs                                                                
       11 -recorded in a lockfile. The only macOS-specific piece is two tiny  
       12 -compatibility headers injected at compile time via `CPATH` — upstre
          -am                                                                 
       13 -sources are never edited, and `klipperformac doctor` verifies every
          - checkout is

       13  ## Quick start
       14  
       15  ```sh
       16  curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/YOURHANDLE/MaKlipper/m
           ain/scripts/install.sh | sh
       17  export PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"   # once, if prompted
       18  
       22 -klipperformac setup     # fetches upstream components + verifies th
          -ey build on your Mac                                               
       23 -klipperformac serial    # plug in the printer board first; lists US
          -B ports                                                            
       24 -klipperformac serial --auto    # write the found port into printer.
          -cfg                                                                
       25 -klipperformac up        # starts klipper + moonraker + mainsail, ke
          -eps the Mac awake                                                  
       19 +klipperformac setup          # fetch upstream components, build, ve
          +rify                                                               
       20 +klipperformac serial --auto  # plug in the printer board first     
       21 +klipperformac up             # start everything, keep the Mac awake
       22  ```

       34  | command | what it does |
       35  |---|---|
       42 -| `setup` | clone pinned upstream Klipper/Moonraker, download Mains
          -ail + Fluidd, build venv, verify the C engine compiles on this Mac 
          -|                                                                  
       43 -| `up` / `down` / `restart` | start/stop the whole stack (also runs
          - `caffeinate` so sleep can't kill a print) |                       
       44 -| `status` | which services are alive, pinned versions, available u
          -pstream updates |

          -s `[mcu] serial:` in printer.cfg |

          ---use NAME`, `--import PATH` (auto-backup as printer.cfg.bak) |

       56  ## How it works
       57  
       74 -Klipper's host software is nearly portable C + Python already: on m
          -acOS only                                                          
       75 -two system headers it expects (`<linux/can.h>` for CAN framing, `<s
          -ys/prctl.h>`                                                       
       76 -for thread naming) don't exist. MaKlipper ships two 20-line stub he
          -aders and                                                          
       77 -sets `CPATH` at build time so the compiler finds them first. Upstre
          -am sources                                                         
       78 -are bit-for-bit unchanged — `git status` in the checkout proves it.
       58 +Klipper's host is nearly portable C + Python already; on macOS only
          + two system                                                        
       59 +headers it expects (`<linux/can.h>`, `<sys/prctl.h>`) are missing. 
          +MaKlipper                                                          
       60 +ships two ~20-line stubs and sets `CPATH` so the compiler finds the
          +m first.                                                           
       61 +Timing is comfortable: Klipper allows hundreds of milliseconds of h
          +ost latency,                                                       
       62 +and macOS delivers sub-millisecond timer precision. The one rule: *
          +*the Mac                                                           
       63 +must stay awake while printing** — `up` runs `caffeinate` for you; 
          +keep it on AC.                                                     
       64  
       80 -Timing: Klipper's host needs responses within a few hundred millise
          -conds.                                                             
       81 -macOS delivers sub-millisecond timer precision while awake, better 
          -than a                                                             
       82 -stressed Raspberry Pi. The one real rule: **the Mac must stay awake
          - during                                                            
       83 -printing** — `klipperformac up` runs `caffeinate` for you. Closing 
          -the lid or                                                         
       84 -draining the battery can still interrupt a print; keep it on AC pow
          -er.                                                                
       85 -                                                                   
       65  ## Limitations
       66  
       88 -- Running Klipper on macOS is **not supported by the Klipper projec
          -t**                                                                
       89 -  (their stance: "possible but unsupported"). MaKlipper makes it "p
          -ossible"                                                           
       90 -  conveniently — bugs in Klipper itself still go upstream.         
       91 -- Shaper / input-shaping calibration (`SHAPER_CALIBRATE`) needs Pyt
          -hon 3.14                                                           
       92 -  (macOS' multiprocessing default otherwise breaks it); use        
       93 -  `MAKLIPPER_PYTHON=/path/to/python3.14 klipperformac setup` when p
          -ossible.                                                           
       94 -- `CAN bus`, host GPIO, KlipperScreen, and webcam integration are L
          -inux-only                                                          
       95 -  or not wired up yet on v1.                                       
       96 -- Notarized `.app` packaging is the planned v2; v1 is the CLI + Mai
          -nsail.                                                             
       67 +- Not supported by the Klipper project ("possible but unsupported")
          +; Klipper                                                          
       68 +  bugs still go upstream.                                          
       69 +- `SHAPER_CALIBRATE` needs Python 3.14: `MAKLIPPER_PYTHON=…3.14 kli
          +pperformac setup`.                                                 
       70 +- No CAN bus, host GPIO, KlipperScreen, or webcam in v1.           
       71 +- Notarized one-click `.app` is planned v2; v1 is CLI + TUI + web d
          +ashboard.

     def _download(url, dest_path, timeout=60, max_bytes=200 * 1024 * 1024):

                 open(dest_path, "wb") as f:

         finally:

                 "compat shims.\n" + detail)

     def setup(verbose=True):
         pins = load_pins()
         save_pins(pins)
         problems = check_prereqs()
         if problems:
             raise SystemExit("MaKlipper setup requires:\n  - " + "\n  -
     ".join(problems))
         if verbose:
             print("Fetching upstream components (pristine checkouts, pinned
     refs)...")
         for comp in ("klipper", "moonraker"):
             sha = checkout(comp, pins[comp])
             pins[comp + "_sha"] = sha
                 print("[!] {} checkout is not pristine:\n{}".format(comp,
     out))
         elif verbose:
             print("  upstream checkouts verified pristine (git status clean)")
         if verbose:
             print("\nAll components fetched from upstream and verified to
     build on "
                   "this Mac.\nNext: connect your printer board, run maklipper 
     "               "serial, then maklipper up.")
         return pins

     def _get_json(url):

     "application/vnd.github+json"}

     #!/bin/sh
     klipperformac launcher — works straight from a git checkout or via 
     symlink.

     (bin/maklipper remains as a legacy alias to the same app.)

     SELF="$0"
     while [ -L "$SELF" ]; do
         L=$(readlink "$SELF")
         case "$L" in
             /*) SELF="$L" ;;
             *)  SELF=$(dirname "$SELF")/"$L" ;;
         esac
     done
     DIR=$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$SELF")/.." && pwd)
     cd "$DIR" || exit 1
     PY="${MAKLIPPER_PYTHON:-python3}"
     PYTHONPATH="$DIR${PYTHONPATH:+:$PYTHONPATH}" exec "$PY" -m maklipper "$@"

     ---install.sh---
     #!/bin/sh
     curl-friendly one-liner installer for maklipper (CLI only, no GUI).

     curl -fsSL 
     https://raw.githubusercontent.com//MaKlipper/main/scripts/install.sh | sh

     set -e

     REPO="${MAKLIPPER_REPO:-}"
     SRC_DIR="${MAKLIPPER_SRC:-$HOME/.maklipper/src/MaKlipper}"
     BIN_DIR="${MAKLIPPER_BIN_DIR:-$HOME/.local/bin}"

     say() { printf 'klipperformac-install: %s\n' "$1"; }

     Repo source: explicit env, or the repo this script was run from (git

     checkout), or the configured origin — refuse to guess placeholders.

     if [ -z "$REPO" ]; then
       for cand in "$PWD" "$PWD/../.."; do
         if [ -d "$cand/.git" ]; then
           REPO=$(git -C "$cand" remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null || true)
           [ -n "$REPO" ] && break
         fi
       done
     fi
     if [ -z "$REPO" ] || echo "$REPO" | grep -q "YOURHANDLE|"; then
       say "ERROR: set MAKLIPPER_REPO=https://github.com//MaKlipper
     (placeholder repo URL)."
       exit 1
     fi

     Prechecks.

     command -v git  >/dev/null 2>&1 || { say "git not found. Run: xcode-select
      --install"; exit 1; }
     command -v cc   >/dev/null 2>&1 || { say "compiler not found. Run:
     xcode-select --install"; exit 1; }
     python3 -c 'import sys; sys.exit(0 if sys.version_info >= (3, 8) else 1)'
     2>/dev/null
       || { say "python3 >= 3.8 required for the CLI itself"; exit 1; }

     Fetch or update the app sources.

     mkdir -p "$(dirname "$SRC_DIR")"
     if [ -d "$SRC_DIR/.git" ]; then
       say "updating existing checkout"
       if ! git -C "$SRC_DIR" pull --ff-only --quiet; then
         say "WARNING: checkout at $SRC_DIR is dirty/diverged; leaving as-is."
       fi
     else
       say "cloning $REPO"
       git clone --quiet "$REPO" "$SRC_DIR"
     fi

     Install the launcher.

     mkdir -p "$BIN_DIR"
     ln -sf "$SRC_DIR/bin/maklipper" "$BIN_DIR/maklipper"
     say "installed maklipper -> $BIN_DIR/maklipper"

     case ":$PATH:" in
       ":$BIN_DIR:") ;;
       *) say "NOTE: add $BIN_DIR to your PATH, e.g. in ~/.zshrc:"
          say "  export PATH=$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH" ;;
     esac

     say "next step: klipperformac setup"

          -pstream updates |

          -data in

       56  ## How it works
       57  
       74 -Klipper's host software is nearly portable C + Python already: on m
          -acOS only                                                          
       75 -two system headers it expects (`<linux/can.h>` for CAN framing, `<s
          -ys/prctl.h>`                                                       
       76 -for thread naming) don't exist. MaKlipper ships two 20-line stub he
          -aders and                                                          
       77 -sets `CPATH` at build time so the compiler finds them first. Upstre
          -am sources                                                         
       78 -are bit-for-bit unchanged — `git status` in the checkout proves it.
       58 +Klipper's host is nearly portable C + Python already; on macOS only
          + two system                                                        
       59 +headers it expects (`<linux/can.h>`, `<sys/prctl.h>`) are missing. 
          +MaKlipper                                                          
       60 +ships two ~20-line stubs and sets `CPATH` so the compiler finds the
          +m first.                                                           
       61 +Timing is comfortable: Klipper allows hundreds of milliseconds of h
          +ost latency,                                                       
       62 +and macOS delivers sub-millisecond timer precision. The one rule: *
          +*the Mac                                                           
       63 +must stay awake while printing** — `up` runs `caffeinate` for you; 
          +keep it on AC.                                                     
       64  
       80 -Timing: Klipper's host needs responses within a few hundred millise
          -conds.                                                             
       81 -macOS delivers sub-millisecond timer precision while awake, better 
          -than a                                                             
       82 -stressed Raspberry Pi. The one real rule: **the Mac must stay awake
          - during                                                            
       83 -printing** — `klipperformac up` runs `caffeinate` for you. Closing 
          -the lid or                                                         
       84 -draining the battery can still interrupt a print; keep it on AC pow
          -er.                                                                
       85 -                                                                   
       65  ## Limitations
       66  
       88 -- Running Klipper on macOS is **not supported by the Klipper projec
          -t**                                                                
       89 -  (their stance: "possible but unsupported"). MaKlipper makes it "p
          -ossible"                                                           
       90 -  conveniently — bugs in Klipper itself still go upstream.         
       91 -- Shaper / input-shaping calibration (`SHAPER_CALIBRATE`) needs Pyt
          -hon 3.14                                                           
       92 -  (macOS' multiprocessing default otherwise breaks it); use        
       93 -  `MAKLIPPER_PYTHON=/path/to/python3.14 klipperformac setup` when p
          -ossible.                                                           
       94 -- `CAN bus`, host GPIO, KlipperScreen, and webcam integration are L
          -inux-only                                                          
       95 -  or not wired up yet on v1.                                       
       96 -- Notarized `.app` packaging is the planned v2; v1 is the CLI + Mai
          -nsail.                                                             
       67 +- Not supported by the Klipper project ("possible but unsupported")
          +; Klipper                                                          
       68 +  bugs still go upstream.                                          
       69 +- `SHAPER_CALIBRATE` needs Python 3.14: `MAKLIPPER_PYTHON=…3.14 kli
          +pperformac setup`.                                                 
       70 +- No CAN bus, host GPIO, KlipperScreen, or webcam in v1.           
       71 +- Notarized one-click `.app` is planned v2; v1 is CLI + TUI + web d
          +ashboard.

     ---bin---
     #!/bin/sh
     klipperformac launcher — works straight from a git checkout or via 
     symlink.

     (bin/maklipper remains as a legacy alias to the same app.)

     SELF="$0"
     while [ -L "$SELF" ]; do
         L=$(readlink "$SELF")
         case "$L" in
             /*) SELF="$L" ;;
             *)  SELF=$(dirname "$SELF")/"$L" ;;
         esac
     done
     DIR=$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$SELF")/.." && pwd)
     cd "$DIR" || exit 1
     PY="${MAKLIPPER_PYTHON:-python3}"
     PYTHONPATH="$DIR${PYTHONPATH:+:$PYTHONPATH}" exec "$PY" -m maklipper "$@"

     ---install.sh---
     #!/bin/sh
     curl-friendly one-liner installer for maklipper (CLI only, no GUI).

     curl -fsSL 
     https://raw.githubusercontent.com//MaKlipper/main/scripts/install.sh | sh

     set -e

     REPO="${MAKLIPPER_REPO:-}"
     SRC_DIR="${MAKLIPPER_SRC:-$HOME/.maklipper/src/MaKlipper}"
     BIN_DIR="${MAKLIPPER_BIN_DIR:-$HOME/.local/bin}"

     say() { printf 'klipperformac-install: %s\n' "$1"; }

     Repo source: explicit env, or the repo this script was run from (git

     checkout), or the configured origin — refuse to guess placeholders.

     if [ -z "$REPO" ]; then
       for cand in "$PWD" "$PWD/../.."; do
         if [ -d "$cand/.git" ]; then
           REPO=$(git -C "$cand" remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null || true)
           [ -n "$REPO" ] && break
         fi
       done
     fi
     if [ -z "$REPO" ] || echo "$REPO" | grep -q "YOURHANDLE|"; then
       say "ERROR: set MAKLIPPER_REPO=https://github.com//MaKlipper
     (placeholder repo URL)."
       exit 1
     fi

     Prechecks.

     command -v git  >/dev/null 2>&1 || { say "git not found. Run: xcode-select
      --install"; exit 1; }
     command -v cc   >/dev/null 2>&1 || { say "compiler not found. Run:
     xcode-select --install"; exit 1; }
     python3 -c 'import sys; sys.exit(0 if sys.version_info >= (3, 8) else 1)'
     2>/dev/null
       || { say "python3 >= 3.8 required for the CLI itself"; exit 1; }

     Fetch or update the app sources.

     mkdir -p "$(dirname "$SRC_DIR")"
     if [ -d "$SRC_DIR/.git" ]; then
       say "updating existing checkout"
       if ! git -C "$SRC_DIR" pull --ff-only --quiet; then
         say "WARNING: checkout at $SRC_DIR is dirty/diverged; leaving as-is."
       fi
     else
       say "cloning $REPO"
       git clone --quiet "$REPO" "$SRC_DIR"
     fi

     Install the launcher.

     mkdir -p "$BIN_DIR"
     ln -sf "$SRC_DIR/bin/maklipper" "$BIN_DIR/maklipper"
     say "installed maklipper -> $BIN_DIR/maklipper"

     case ":$PATH:" in
       ":$BIN_DIR:") ;;
       *) say "NOTE: add $BIN_DIR to your PATH, e.g. in ~/.zshrc:"
          say "  export PATH=$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH" ;;
     esac

     say "next step: klipperformac setup"

   vbin = Path("~/.klipperformac/venv/bin").expanduser()

           t = f.read_text()
       except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
           continue
       if ".maklipper" in t:
           f.write_text(t.replace(".maklipper", ".klipperformac"))
           fixed.append(f.name)
   print("repaired:", sorted(fixed))

     def _patch_ui_config(component, dest):
         """Point the UIs at their own proxied origin: klipperformac.proxy
     relays
         REST + websocket to Moonraker on the same port, so defaults work."""
         cfg = dest / "config.json"
         try:
             data = json.loads(cfg.read_text())
         except Exception:
             return
         if component == "mainsail":
             data["hostname"] = None
             data["port"] = None
         elif component == "fluidd":
             data["socket_url"] = "ws://localhost:{}/websocket".format(
                 paths.FLUIDD_PORT)
         cfg.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2) + "\n")

     def fetch_mainsail(tag):
         fetch_webui("mainsail", tag, paths.WEB_MAINSAIL)

     def fetch_fluidd(tag):
         fetch_webui("fluidd", tag, paths.WEB_FLUIDD)

         """Compile + load c_helper.so exactly the way klippy will at first
     run."""

     def _patch_ui_config(component, dest):
         """Point the UIs at their own proxied origin: klipperformac.proxy
     relays
         REST + websocket to Moonraker on the same port, so defaults work."""
         cfg = dest / "config.json"
         try:
             data = json.loads(cfg.read_text())
         except Exception:
             return
         if component == "mainsail":
             data["hostname"] = None
             data["port"] = None
         elif component == "fluidd":
             data["socket_url"] = "ws://localhost:{}/websocket".format(
                 paths.FLUIDD_PORT)
         cfg.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2) + "\n")

     def fetch_mainsail(tag):
         fetch_webui("mainsail", tag, paths.WEB_MAINSAIL)

     def fetch_fluidd(tag):
         fetch_webui("fluidd", tag, paths.WEB_FLUIDD)

     def test_chelper_build():
         """Compile + load c_helper.so exactly the way klippy will at first
     run."""
